Operating rules

Order Management and GSS coordinate shipping, receiving and deployment through Stages 7–8. Stage 7 is the confirmed/booked PO with locked booked facts and Close Date; Stage 8 Invoice is reached when the complete in-scope order has been delivered on site. Shipment alone does not prove receipt. OM verifies receipt with GSS against every non-cancelled booked item; partial deliveries remain in 7 and approved scope changes are recorded on the Order. Keep promised/scheduled ship dates, actual shipping, receipt and planned/actual deployment distinct on the Order/Case. Record and reconcile invoice evidence on the existing Order and Opportunity references; financial invoice date remains distinct from on-site delivery. Do not create a new Opportunity deployment-status or completion-date field. Applicability to confirm — OM and GSS must define the logistics dates, deployment-Case requirement and Stage 8 completion evidence for renewals, software/services, Internal Transfer, Non-Revenue and No Shipping required orders. The physical-delivery path applies where shipping/deployment is in scope. Do not invent delivery dates, create a dummy Case or treat shipment as receipt to satisfy it. Confirm the applicable alternative before enabling universal logistics/Case validation; no new exception status or field is approved here. Verified complete on-site delivery controls entry to Stage 8; pending financial invoicing does not block that transition. Invoice Reference and the related Order Invoice Date are required when the invoice is issued, not before delivery-led completion. OM continues invoice reconciliation on the Order after Stage 8; retain all related invoice references and dates. Do not enter placeholder invoice values or use financial Invoice Date as the delivery/time-to-value milestone. OM must confirm order reconciliation before 6 → 7; a nonblank Order Review Status is not sufficient. Configuration prerequisite: map the existing processing, Booked-Complete, booked-with-exception, conditional/demo and revised-PO states to allowed/blocked transitions, with the required exception evidence and authority. RevOps and OM must approve that mapping before enabling the booking gate; do not silently accept every Booked value or invent new picklist choices.

All ongoing activities ↑

Automations and permissions

Order Management controls booking and order reconciliation; Finance maintains authorized invoice facts. Sales, partner overlays and GSS receive only the order context needed for their duties. Booked opportunity facts stay locked.

Access: role permissions and automation controls.

Stages 6–8 · GSS + Order Management

Deployment case

Process

Create or link the coordinating PS deployment Case at Won decision (6), using the existing Opportunity PS Case and Order Case(WO) relationships after confirming their targets. GSS starts deployment planning with Sales/SE and OM in Stage 6. Before booking at 7, identify the GSS case owner and document shipping, on-site receiving and installation/deployment plans, dates, responsibilities and dependencies on the Case and linked Order. Avoid duplicate cases on stage re-entry; associate later Orders with the same coordinating case and retain subordinate workstream cases where needed. Deliver the Deal continues on the PS Case, not as numbered Opportunity stages. GSS manages delivery/setup, the deployment checklist and completion evidence using existing Case fields and workstream relationships; confirm the exact status/date mappings before configuration. The coordinating Case covers all in-scope workstreams; closing one case alone does not prove full deployment. Track the sales-defined value plan, target timing, achieved Yes/No, assessment date and supporting context on the Case. Pending outcome stays blank; No requires the gap and agreed disposition. GSS uses results to inform renewals and upsell through separately linked sales opportunities. The original opportunity ends at Stage 8 and is never held open, advanced or reopened to wait for deployment or value realization.

Connected fields

Case automations and validations

Store Value Tracking Start Date on the coordinating PS Case. Preserve the first Stage 8 entry / verified on-site-delivery milestone from the linked Opportunity once; do not reset it on re-entry, retries or case reassignment. This retains the agreed delivery-based time-to-value baseline, not the financial Invoice Date or the Case creation date at Stage 6. On the Case, calculate Time to Value only when Value Achieved? = Yes and Value Outcome Confirmed Date is on/after that start; pending/No stays blank. A missing start is an integration exception, not a reason to reopen or hold the Opportunity.

Require a plan and target date for value tracking; require outcome, assessment date and context only when GSS completes the value assessment. Do not equate a deployment Case closure with value achievement. Preserve all workstream evidence and the original booked Opportunity.

Operating rules

Order Management and GSS coordinate shipping, receiving and deployment through Stages 7–8. Stage 7 is the confirmed/booked PO with locked booked facts and Close Date; Stage 8 Invoice is reached when the complete in-scope order has been delivered on site. Shipment alone does not prove receipt. OM verifies receipt with GSS against every non-cancelled booked item; partial deliveries remain in 7 and approved scope changes are recorded on the Order. Keep promised/scheduled ship dates, actual shipping, receipt and planned/actual deployment distinct on the Order/Case. Record and reconcile invoice evidence on the existing Order and Opportunity references; financial invoice date remains distinct from on-site delivery. Do not create a new Opportunity deployment-status or completion-date field. Store Value Tracking Start Date on the coordinating PS Case. Preserve the first Stage 8 entry / verified on-site-delivery milestone from the linked Opportunity once; do not reset it on re-entry, retries or case reassignment. This retains the agreed delivery-based time-to-value baseline, not the financial Invoice Date or the Case creation date at Stage 6. On the Case, calculate Time to Value only when Value Achieved? = Yes and Value Outcome Confirmed Date is on/after that start; pending/No stays blank. A missing start is an integration exception, not a reason to reopen or hold the Opportunity. Applicability to confirm — OM and GSS must define the logistics dates, deployment-Case requirement and Stage 8 completion evidence for renewals, software/services, Internal Transfer, Non-Revenue and No Shipping required orders. The physical-delivery path applies where shipping/deployment is in scope. Do not invent delivery dates, create a dummy Case or treat shipment as receipt to satisfy it. Confirm the applicable alternative before enabling universal logistics/Case validation; no new exception status or field is approved here.

All ongoing activities ↑

Automations and permissions

GSS owns deployment status, completion and value-realization evidence on the PS Case. Sales and SEs contribute customer/technical context; Finance reconciles authorized order facts. Case access and updates do not reopen or edit booked opportunity results.

Access: role permissions and automation controls.

Outside the one-page summary

Optional opp fields

Additional customer, competitor, solution, operations and system context. Review the Inputs column for dropdown choices and character limits. These fields do not introduce new stage-exit requirements; existing platform requirements and system ownership remain in force.

Existing fulfillment and service fields are retained on their respective objects: see the Order inventory and PS Case inventory. They do not become additional Opportunity fields or stage requirements.

POC StatusPicklist · proposedBR-SS-008, BR-SS-074
Proposed inputs

None / Planning / In Progress / Blocked / Cancelled / Completed

No existing field confirmed; proposed capture.New fieldDefault None. Track Planning, In Progress, Blocked, Cancelled or Completed. On any forward transition beyond Stage 2: POC Required? = No may proceed; Yes requires POC Status = Cancelled or Completed. Yes with None, Planning, In Progress or Blocked cannot proceed. Require a Yes/No decision before Stage 3. Apply to stage skips as well as 2 → 3.

Value Tracking Start DateDate/time · PS CaseBR-SS-018, BR-SS-058
Proposed inputs

Proposed field; no current input control. Use the proposed type shown above.

No existing field confirmed; proposed capture.New fieldObject: PS CaseCase system stamp · first Opportunity Stage 8 entryStore Value Tracking Start Date on the coordinating PS Case. On the Case, calculate Time to Value only when Value Achieved? = Yes and Value Outcome Confirmed Date is on/after that start; pending/No stays blank. A missing start is an integration exception, not a reason to reopen or hold the Opportunity.

Opportunity Conversion LifecyclePicklist · system-maintained · proposedBR-SS-110
Proposed inputs

Proposed field; no current input control. Use the proposed type shown above.

Proposed inputs
  • SAL
  • SQL
No existing field confirmed; proposed capture.New fieldSystem-maintained · SAL at creation / SQL at qualified pipeline entryNew Opportunity-level conversion milestone: SAL on creation; SQL at first qualified pipeline entry, including permitted skips and seller-direct Stage 1 creation.
Full rule

Separate from sales Stage and existing Contact/Account lifecycle. Retain source, qualification channel (MAP / PAX.AI / seller / partner / BDR), event timestamp, actor/system, Contact, Account and Opportunity relationship in conversion history. Reuse suitable existing lifecycle history/event storage, extending only missing data. Record MQL or AQL at Contact qualification; SAL at opportunity creation; accepted/rejected decision separately; SQL on first qualified entry to Stages 1–8, including permitted skips. Preserve every episode for multiple opportunities or requalification and deduplicate retries with the source event/episode ID. Late MQL/AQL messages, stage regression and a new Stage 0 opportunity must not downgrade a Contact or Account already further through its lifecycle. Keep opportunity-level events so separate deals remain reportable. Report MQL→SAL→SQL and AQL→SAL→SQL as separate cohorts, including rejected/pending SALs and elapsed time. Seller-direct SQLs remain a separate cohort; never inflate inbound conversion by fabricating MQL/AQL history.

Full rule

GSS starts deployment planning with Sales/SE and OM in Stage 6.
